# Skills · D · part 28

929 entries, debate-decisions to debug. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **debate-decisions**: A compact decision protocol for non-trivial choices, reviews, governance calls, and live disagreement. Use implicitly at decision points, even when nobody says "debate", whenever a choice is high-impact, hard to reverse, policy-bearing, or has multiple plausible paths that evidence alone cannot settle. Skip for trivial, cheap-to-reverse, or deterministically decidable choices. ([ariaxhan/the-agent-library](https://github.com/ariaxhan/the-agent-library/tree/HEAD/process-engines/debate-decisions) · ★2)
- **debate-facilitation**: 多空辩论主持专家。桥水 AIA 辩论制的治理化（随 bundles/trading 分发）：组织 Bull/Bear 双方对灰区标的产出证据卡，Coordinator 汇总为交易卡片第六段。安装后被 debate-coordinator 调用；铁律——辩论只产证据，绝不修改任何分数与闸门输出。 ([geniusdapeng-collab/workroom-tiger](https://github.com/geniusdapeng-collab/workroom-tiger/tree/HEAD/governance/bundles/trading/skills/debate-facilitation))
- **debate-facilitator**: Neutral moderation for structured debates among multiple agents. Use when Codex must coordinate agent perspectives, expose disagreement, manage consensus checks, document dissent, produce debate minutes, create decision records, handle deadlock, or turn multi-agent discussion into a traceable decision. ([koala-man-64/helpful-scripts](https://github.com/koala-man-64/helpful-scripts/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/debate-facilitator) · 3 copies)
